(96)

where
\overset{\circ}{\piup}_{1}(-)=-|_{\partial M}
which is uniquely determined by the tangential component
\omega^{\|}
(although they strictly speaking belong to different spaces) and so for all
\omega\in CcC^{p}_{N}
we may formally identify
\omega|_{p\in\partial M}=\omega^{\|}=\omega|_{\partial M}
. The higher products follow from the homological perturbation lemma as discussed in
Section

2.4
and give a perturbative expansion of the classical solutions given specified boundary data.

3.2.2
Yang–Mills theory

Finally, let us discuss Yang–Mills theory in four dimensions, including a topological
\theta
-term, in the framework of relative
L_{\infty}
-algebras. We work with the usual second-order formulation.
21
21

21

For first-order formulations see e.g.
[
33
,
14
,
3
]
.

Relative
L_{\infty}
-algebra.

We take the colour Lie algebra of the theory to be an ordinary finite-dimensional metric Lie algebra
(V,[-,-]_{V},\langle-,-\rangle_{V})
. Correspondingly, the relative
L_{\infty}
-algebra of Yang–Mills theory on an oriented compact four-dimensional Riemannian manifold
(M,g)
with boundary
\partial M
is given by

{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overbrace{\Omegaup^{0}(M,V)}^{\in\,c}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overbrace{\Omegaup^{1}(M,V)}^{\in\,A}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overbrace{\Omegaup^{3}(M,V)}^{\in\,A^{+}}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\overbrace{\Omegaup^{4}(M,V)}^{\in\,c^{+}}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\underbrace{\Omegaup^{0}(\partial M,V)}_{\in\,\gamma}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\underbrace{\big(\Omegaup^{1}(\partial M,V)\oplus\Omegaup^{2}(\partial M,V)\big)}_{\in\,(\alpha,\beta)}}
{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\underbrace{\Omegaup^{3}(\partial M,V)}_{\in\,\alpha^{+}}.}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\piup_{1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\piup_{1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\piup_{1}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{1}^{\partial}}
\scriptstyle{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\mu_{1}^{\partial}}

(97a)

where the first row represents
V
and the second row
V_{\partial}
. Notice that there is the extra component
\Omegaup^{2}(\partial M,\mathfrak{g})
in the boundary
L_{\infty}
-algebra
V_{\partial}
labelled by
\beta
). The reason for this is that only the gauge potential
A\in\Omegaup^{1}(M,V)
appears with second-order terms in the Yang–Mills action. Furthermore, upon letting ‘
*
’ be the Hodge operator with respect to the metric
g
, we have (see e.g.
[
11
,
12
,
14
]
)

\begin{gathered}\mu_{1}(c)\coloneqq\mathrm{d}_{M}c,\quad\mu_{1}(A)\coloneqq\mathrm{d}_{M}{*\mathrm{d}_{M}A},\quad\mu_{1}(A^{+})\coloneqq\mathrm{d}_{M}A^{+},\\
\mu_{2}(c,c^{\prime})\coloneqq[c,c^{\prime}]_{V},\quad\mu_{2}(c,A)\coloneqq[c,A]_{V},\quad\mu_{2}(c,A^{+})\coloneqq[c,A^{+}]_{V},\quad\mu_{2}(c,c^{\prime+})\coloneqq[c,c^{\prime+}]_{V},\\
\mu_{2}(A,A^{\prime})\coloneqq\mathrm{d}_{M}{*[A,A^{\prime}]_{V}}+[A,{*\mathrm{d}_{M}A^{\prime}}]_{V}+[A^{\prime},{*\mathrm{d}_{M}A}]_{V},\quad\mu_{2}(A,A^{\prime+})\coloneqq[A,A^{\prime+}]_{V},\\
\mu_{3}(A,A^{\prime},A^{\prime\prime})\coloneqq[A,{*[A^{\prime},A^{\prime\prime}]_{V}}]_{V}+\text{cyclic}\end{gathered}

(97b)

as well as

\begin{gathered}\mu_{1}^{\partial}(\gamma)\coloneqq\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{d}_{\partial M}\gamma\\
0\end{pmatrix},\quad\mu_{1}^{\partial}\begin{pmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\end{pmatrix}\coloneqq\mathrm{d}_{\partial M}\beta,\\
\mu_{2}^{\partial}(\gamma,\gamma^{\prime})\coloneqq[\gamma,\gamma^{\prime}]_{V},\quad\mu_{2}^{\partial}\left(\gamma,\begin{pmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\end{pmatrix}\right)\coloneqq\begin{pmatrix}[\gamma,\alpha]_{V}\\
[\gamma,\beta]_{V}\end{pmatrix},\quad\mu_{2}^{\partial}(\gamma,\alpha^{+})\coloneqq[\gamma,\alpha^{+}]_{V},\\
\mu_{2}^{\partial}\left(\begin{pmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\end{pmatrix},\begin{pmatrix}\alpha^{\prime}\\
\beta^{\prime}\end{pmatrix}\right)\coloneqq[\alpha,\beta^{\prime},]_{V}+[\alpha^{\prime},\beta]_{V}\end{gathered}

(97c)

and

\begin{gathered}\piup_{1}(c)\coloneqq c|_{\partial M},\quad\piup_{1}(A)\coloneqq\left.\begin{pmatrix}A\\
*\mathrm{d}_{M}A+\theta\mathrm{d}_{M}A\end{pmatrix}\right|_{\partial M},\quad\piup_{1}(A^{+})\coloneqq A^{+}|_{\partial M},\\
\piup_{2}(A,A^{\prime})\coloneqq\left.\begin{pmatrix}0\\
{*[A,A^{\prime}]_{V}}+\theta[A,A^{\prime}]_{V}\end{pmatrix}\right|_{\partial M}.\end{gathered}

(97d)

In addition, we introduce the bilinear forms that have the non-vanishing components

\begin{gathered}\langle c,c^{\prime+}\rangle\coloneqq\int_{M}\langle c,c^{\prime+}\rangle_{V}\eqqcolon\langle c^{\prime+},c\rangle_{V},\quad\langle A,A^{\prime+}\rangle\coloneqq\int_{M}\langle A,A^{\prime+}\rangle_{V}\eqqcolon\langle A^{\prime+},A\rangle_{V},\\
\langle\gamma,\alpha^{+}\rangle_{\partial}\coloneqq\int_{\partial M}\langle\gamma,\alpha^{+}\rangle_{V},\quad\left\langle\begin{pmatrix}\alpha\\
\beta\end{pmatrix},\begin{pmatrix}\alpha^{\prime}\\
\beta^{\prime}\end{pmatrix}\right\rangle_{\partial}\coloneqq\int_{\partial M}\langle\alpha,\beta^{\prime}\rangle_{V}.\end{gathered}

(97e)

Then, the relative homotopy Maurer–Cartan action (
25
) becomes

\displaystyle S

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{M}\Big\{\langle A,\mathrm{d}_{M}{*\mathrm{d}_{M}A}\rangle_{V}+\langle A^{+},\mathrm{d}_{M}c\rangle_{V}-\langle c,\mathrm{d}_{M}A^{+}\rangle_{V}\Big\}

(98)

\displaystyle+\int_{M}\Big\{\tfrac{1}{3!}\langle A,\mathrm{d}_{M}{*[A,A]_{V}}+2[A,{*\mathrm{d}_{M}A}]_{V}\rangle_{V}+\langle A^{+},[A,c]_{V}\rangle_{V}+\tfrac{1}{2}\langle c^{+},[c,c]_{V}\rangle_{V}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\int_{M}\langle A,[A,{*[A,A]_{V}}]_{V}\rangle_{V}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\partial M}\Big\{\langle c,A^{+}\rangle_{V}+\langle A,{*\mathrm{d}_{M}A}+\theta\mathrm{d}_{M}A\rangle_{V}\Big\}\Big|_{\partial M}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{3!}\int_{\partial M}\langle A,{*[A,A]}_{V}+\theta[A,A]_{V}\rangle_{V}\Big|_{\partial M}

\displaystyle=\int_{M}\Big\{\tfrac{1}{2}\langle F,{*F}\rangle_{V}+\langle\nabla_{M}A^{+},c\rangle_{V}+\tfrac{1}{2}\langle c^{+},[c,c]_{V}\rangle_{V}+\tfrac{\theta}{2}\langle F,F\rangle_{V}\Big\},

where, as before,
F\coloneqq\mathrm{d}_{M}A+\frac{1}{2}[A,A]_{V}
and
\nabla_{M}c\coloneqq\mathrm{d}_{M}c+[A,c]_{V}
.
